Valve's detail file for the 6666 standing at #127 lists amster, dENZY, kL1o, rendysky and skcH on 923.2 points; the HLTV fixture for CCT Europe 2026 Challengers #8 lists the team labelled ex-6666 as skcH, kL1o, amster, rendysky and stefienson, four shared players. The standings' other row labelled 6666 (#368, 424.5 points; auth0ri, m4d4ra, rickangel, skcH) shares one, so the overlap selects exactly one row and the model still refuses the label if the overlap or the uniqueness test fails. Scope: CS2 main-team label only, and an exact opponent pair plus UTC start is still required for any fixture."
